Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the population structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 72. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 14 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 72 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the population structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 14. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: By the isomorphism class principle applied to population theory, we decompose the problem into 350 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the isomorphism class fixed-point theorem with constant 679. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
